By the time morning actually gets here, I feel as if I've hardly rested at all.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your appetite over the past eight months?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't really noticed any changes there. I still eat my regular meals, and my appetite seems to be about the same as it always has been, honestly.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your energy level or how you've been moving around over the past eight months?\n\nPatient: No, I can't say that I have. My energy feels pretty much the same as it always does. I mean, even with waking up early like I mentioned, I don't feel physically drained during the day, and I haven't noticed myself moving any slower than usual. I just get up and go about my normal routine around the house.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling guilty about anything in particular over the past eight months?\n\nPatient: Yes, I have. I keep going back over my brother's decline, honestly. I just... I keep telling myself that if I had been a better sister, or just more attentive, I would have noticed what was happening sooner. It's hard not to feel like I could have prevented some of this if I had just paid closer attention to him.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed yourself fidgeting more or feeling restless, or on the other hand, feeling slowed down in your movements or speech over the past eight months?\n\nPatient: No, um... like I mentioned just a minute ago, I really haven't felt slowed down at all. And I haven't been restless or fidgeting, either. My speech and the way I move around... it all seems exactly the same as usual to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt like you're moving more slowly than usual or like your thoughts are slower?\n\nPatient: No, like I just mentioned a moment ago, I really don't feel like I'm moving any slower than usual. And as for my thoughts... no, they don't feel slow either. Everything seems to be working at its normal pace in that regard.\n\nDoctor: Have you lost any significant amount of weight over the past eight months?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. My weight has stayed pretty much the same over the last eight months. I haven't noticed any significant changes at all.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt like you're moving more slowly than usual in your daily activities?\n\nPatient: Well, like I think we just went over a few minutes ago... no, I haven't. I'm really not moving any slower than I normally do when I go about my day. My pace is just the same as it's always been.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt like you're restless or have an inability to sit still over the past eight months?\n\nPatient: No, not at all. I haven't felt restless, and... well, sitting still hasn't been an issue for me. I'm perfectly fine just staying put.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt like your thoughts are racing or speeding up over the past eight months?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't noticed anything like that. My thoughts seem to be going at their normal pace, not racing or anything.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt like you're unable to make decisions or have difficulty concentrating over the past eight months?\n\nPatient: No, I wouldn't say so. It's just there, and I can't quite reason my way out of it, no matter how much I try to rationalize things.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any decrease in pleasure from activities you usually enjoy over the past eight months?\n\nPatient: Yes, I have. I've painted for years, but lately... well, I still set out my watercolor things sometimes, hoping I'll get into it. But I'll just sit there staring at the paper, and eventually, I put it all away. The pleasure I used to feel in the color and the light, it just doesn't seem to arrive anymore.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your sleep pattern over the past eight months?\n\nPatient: Yes, I have. Getting to sleep has been a bit uneven lately. And then... well, I almost always wake up around three or four in the morning. My mind just starts going over phone calls, forms I need to deal with, and just worrying about whether I handled things correctly. By the time morning actually gets here, I feel as if I've hardly rested at all.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your appetite over the past eight months?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't really noticed any changes there. I still eat my regular meals, and my appetite seems to be about the same as it always has been, honestly.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your energy level or how you've been moving around over the past eight months?\n\nPatient: No, I can't say that I have. My energy feels pretty much the same as it always does. I mean, even with waking up early like I mentioned, I don't feel physically drained during the day, and I haven't noticed myself moving any slower than usual. I just get up and go about my normal routine around the house.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling guilty about anything in particular over the past eight months?\n\nPatient: Yes, I have. I keep going back over my brother's decline, honestly. I just... I keep telling myself that if I had been a better sister, or just more attentive, I would have noticed what was happening sooner. It's hard not to feel like I could have prevented some of this if I had just paid closer attention to him.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed yourself fidgeting more or feeling restless, or on the other hand, feeling slowed down in your movements or speech over the past eight months?\n\nPatient: No, um... like I mentioned just a minute ago, I really haven't felt slowed down at all. And I haven't been restless or fidgeting, either. My speech and the way I move around... it all seems exactly the same as usual to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt like you're moving more slowly than usual or like your thoughts are slower?\n\nPatient: No, like I just mentioned a moment ago, I really don't feel like I'm moving any slower than usual. And as for my thoughts... no, they don't feel slow either. Everything seems to be working at its normal pace in that regard.\n\nDoctor: Have you lost any significant amount of weight over the past eight months?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. My weight has stayed pretty much the same over the last eight months. I haven't noticed any significant changes at all.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt like you're moving more slowly than usual in your daily activities?\n\nPatient: Well, like I think we just went over a few minutes ago... no, I haven't. I'm really not moving any slower than I normally do when I go about my day. My pace is just the same as it's always been.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt like you're restless or have an inability to sit still over the past eight months?\n\nPatient: No, not at all. I haven't felt restless, and... well, sitting still hasn't been an issue for me. I'm perfectly fine just staying put.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt like your thoughts are racing or speeding up over the past eight months?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't noticed anything like that. My thoughts seem to be going at their normal pace, not racing or anything.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt like you're unable to make decisions or have difficulty concentrating over the past eight months?\n\nPatient: No, I wouldn't say so. I can still focus on things just fine, like when I'm reading or watching a program. And making decisions hasn't been a problem either. Everything feels pretty normal in that regard.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt like you're unable to sit still or have been fidgeting more often than usual?\n\nPatient: No, I think that's a bit like what you asked earlier.
